April 2018                                          Metal-Enclosed Drawout—Magnum SB
Sheet 21 055
                                                    General Description


Drawout Magnum SB                                                                                                                               i
Switchboard
Eaton’s drawout Magnum SB                                                                                                                       ii
switchboard has been designed
for the power user that desires the
configurations and features of low                                                                                                              1
voltage switchgear, but does not
require the full selectivity and robust-
ness that low voltage metal-enclosed                                                                                                            2
switchgear provides.
The drawout Magnum SB switchboard
is designed and built using many of                                                                                                             3
the same techniques and parts of the
Magnum DS switchgear platform.
The design has been optimized for                                                                                                               4
the differences between the UL 891
standard and the more rigorous
UL 1558 standard.                                                                                                                               5
Refer to Tab 20, Section 20.1 Magnum
DS switchgear structure features,                                                                                                               6
ratings and layout guides. Differences
in these ratings are covered on
the following pages.                                                                                                                            7
Magnum SB is a low voltage insulated
case circuit breaker family designed                                                                                                            8
for the performance and economic
requirements of UL 891 switchboards.
■ Magnum SB insulated case circuit
                                                                                  Magnum SB Switchboard                                         9
  breakers have interruption ratings          Magnum SB breakers meet or exceed                     Drawout Magnum SB switchboards
  up to 100 kA at 635 Vac with                the applicable ANSI, NEMA, UL and                     conform to the following standards:
  continuous current ratings up               CSA standards, including:
                                                                                                                                                10
                                                                                                    ■ CSA
  to 6000 A
                                              ■ ANSI C37.13 (low voltage AC                         ■ UL Standard 891
■ Magnum SB insulated case circuit                                                                                                              11
                                                power circuit breakers used in                      ■ American Bureau of Shipping (ABS)

  time withstand ratings and fixed                                                                  ■ Built in an ISO® certified facility
                                              ■ ANSI C37.16 (preferred ratings,
  internal instantaneous trips on most
                                                related requirements and                            Maximum ratings for Magnum SB               12
  ratings, which is characteristic of
  UL 489 molded case breakers com-              application recommendations for                     switchboards are 600 Vac, 10,000 A
                                                low voltage power circuit breakers                  continuous cross bus and 200 kA
  monly used in UL 891 switchboards.
                                                and AC power circuit breakers)                      short-circuit capacity.                     13
  This provides for greater economy
  and excellent coordination and              ■ ANSI C37.17 (trip devices for AC
  selectivity for most commercial               and general purpose DC low voltage                                                              14
  applications                                  power circuit breakers)

  on all SB insulated case circuit              for low voltage AC power circuit                                                                15

  will provide an extra safety factor         ■ UL 1066 (standard for low voltage

  to downstream circuits at the                 used in enclosures)
  maximum instantaneous trip point
  and facilitate feeder circuit breaker                                                                                                         17
  protection in UL 891 switchboards
  with 3-cycle bus bracing
■ Magnum SBSE current limiting                                                                                                                  18
  power circuit breakers have 150 kA

Construction and Application                                                                                                                     i
Considerations
Eaton’s Pow-R-Line C commercial                                                                                                                  ii
metering switchboards incorporate
design concepts to fit the needs for
reliable multiple circuit distribution                                                                                                           1
and metering in commercial
applications while retaining
maximum standardization, safety                                                                                                                  2
and convenience.
Type WWCMS metering switchboards
provide incoming service and tenant                                                                                                              3
metering arrangements that comply
with EUSERC utility requirements.
                                                                                                                                                 4
Type WCMS metering switchboards
meet the standard requirements
for service and tenant metering                                                                                                                  5
arrangements for all utility locations
other than EUSERC service areas.
Type WCMS may be either hot or                                                                                                                   6
cold sequenced.

9    Meter Socket
     The meter socket is a ringless type
10   device rated 200 A continuous for self-
     contained metering applications. All
     sockets include a manual lever bypass
11   device. The standard meter socket is
     the heavy-duty Type HQ series. Type
     HB sockets are also available.
12   Each meter socket is provided with
     an individual screwless cover that
     includes a sealing bracket with
13   provisions for barrel locks, sealing wire
     or sealing bands. Individual internal
     barriers are provided around each
14   socket.

15

     case circuit breakers, fusible pullout
16   devices (for Class T fuses) or fusible
     switches arranged for Class R fusing.

17   meter socket assembly are established
     based on UL peak let-through currents
     using self-contained meter sockets.
